## Database status

The tracked schema now has 38 application tables and thirty-five migrations. Migration 0034 adds per-set measurement overrides. Migration 0033 adds independent measurement selections. Migrations 0031–0032 add Tasks, projects, schedule links, actual time and independent recurrence templates. Migration 0030 adds saved meals/recipes, ingredient snapshots, favourites and nullable calories. Migration 0029 adds independent workout intent tags, distance/lap measurements and their targets, and two measurement presets. Migration 0028 adds training programs and occurrences. Migration 0027 adds exercise/session tracking types and built-in immutability protection. Migration 0026 adds the reusable-workout draft state and permits incomplete draft exercise lists. Migration 0025 separates private exercise preferences and guards built-in definitions. Migration 0024 adds exercise equipment and muscle arrays. Migration 0023 adds exercise favourites. Migration 0022 adds private catalogue identities. Migration 0021 adds Strength plans, set targets and skipped state. Migration 0020 adds atomic group-movement undo receipts. Migrations 0018–0019 extend repeating plans to all-day items, scoped series changes and reversible snapshots; migration 0017 adds series undo receipts. Migration 0016 adds group-delete undo receipts; migration 0015 adds Routines and extended template ranges; Calendar undo added `deleted_time_blocks` through migrations 0012–0013 and `edited_time_blocks` with series-definition guards in migration 0014. Local development and test migration application succeeded. Admin previously added one table and migrations 0010–0011. Earlier Exercise inspection found 23 tables and ten migrations with zero invalid constraints/indexes and the expected restricted runtime CRUD permissions.

- Development and test databases have migrations 0000–0020 applied. Food adds `0009_exotic_the_hunter.sql`, an index on food-entry history's `(user_id, created_at, id)` columns.
- Development inspection: 23 application tables, ten migrations, zero invalid constraints and zero invalid indexes. The Food history index is present; runtime `hours_app` has required CRUD permissions and cannot create schema objects. Calendar's prior inspection verified its 23 integrity triggers and runtime grants.
- `pnpm db:migrate` succeeded. Final `pnpm db:generate` reported **no schema changes**. Test migrations from an empty schema were also verified during the preceding Calendar pass.
- Verification found an account-deletion cascade conflict in new parent-revision triggers. Migration 0008 fixes it; a PostgreSQL regression now deletes an account containing series/templates and checks that all owned rows disappear.
- Existing local API readiness on 3001 and web login on 3000 returned HTTP 200. No development-data reset was required for the Food pass.

## Checks actually run

Final Exercise `pnpm verify` passed in one aggregate run, including the DST correction:

- Zero-warning lint and persistence/domain import boundaries; formatting; strict workspace typechecks.
- **43 unit tests, six architecture tests and 63 real-PostgreSQL integration tests.**
- Both production builds and **18 browser flows** across desktop, phone and tablet Chromium.
- Browser coverage includes Calendar expansion, prior planning behavior, full Core workflows and known-ID cross-account isolation. Expansion checks include real touch move/resize, exact drag durations, cancellation, Month moves, reviewable range drafts, recurrence, copy/templates, search/filters, quick actions, and a dropped successful response retried with the same key.
- Automated WCAG checks passed. Final desktop/phone/tablet screenshots and the extra 320px layout screenshot were reviewed. Toolbar controls fit without horizontal page overflow.
- Food coverage includes literal search/pagination, restoration and snapshot reuse, fractional portions and unknowns, 23/25-hour daily boundaries, bounded weight reads and ownership. Browser tests cover create-while-logging, food/water/weight logging and corrections, food/weight deletion, lost successful food/water responses with same-key retries, retained stale drafts, unit conversion, date navigation, mobile section links and recoverable read failures. Prior Calendar/Core/isolation flows still pass.
- Exercise coverage includes child-ID validation/stable pagination, restoration, snapshots, filtered ownership, repeat/timed sets, generated-finish retries, stale drafts, explicit past-time corrections, unit conversion, timestamp precision/repeated DST hours, short cardio, recovery and source-time deletion. Exercise desktop/phone/tablet and 320px screenshots were reviewed. A broader run caught a pre-hydration Goals click after a hard reload; the existing Core test now waits for client-loaded records before interacting.

Also ran frozen offline dependency verification using the existing pnpm store (already up to date), focused type/lint/integration/browser checks, `pnpm db:migrate`, `pnpm db:generate`, database health/permission queries and `git diff --check`. Food verification corrected fixture preference initialization, a dialog selector and measurement-history query compatibility. Earlier Calendar fixes for cascades, concurrency and drag/layout behavior remain covered. No manifests or lockfile changes.

Earlier foundation verification also exercised a clean source install/build, migrations against a fresh database, restricted-role provisioning, built-server production cookies/readiness and graceful shutdown. The current integration suite retains startup/failure/shutdown, auth, ownership, transaction and source-history regressions. Local verification used Node 26.0.0. Detailed procedures: [verification](verification.md).

## Deliberate limits and local commands

- Recurrence requires an end within 366 local dates and supports daily/weekly rules. Missing clock times are skipped; repeated clock times use the first occurrence. Clock-change Day views retain exact ordered times and form editing.
- Copy/template operations include at most 100 plans starting in the source day/week. They copy independent plans, not actual entries or recurrence rules. Revise template contents by editing a calendar day/week and capturing a new template.
- Physical-device Safari remains unverified. Fitness sessions autosave with same-tab recovery; reusable workout and exercise forms use explicit saves. New ambiguous/missing local times are rejected; unchanged saved Exercise instants are preserved. Retry state lasts for the current form/action; durable receipts remain until account deletion/reset.
- Food remains manual: no recipes, barcode/provider lookup or custom targets. Weight charts show up to 200 points across 30 days; full history remains paginated.
- Rate limits are process-local; proxy trust is empty by default. Public deployment, recovery/verification email delivery and further feature-slice passes remain separate work. No commit or deployment was performed.

Fresh local setup, with Node 22.12+, pnpm 9.15.9 and Docker Compose:

```bash
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
pnpm setup:env
pnpm db:up
pnpm db:migrate
pnpm db:provision
pnpm dev
```

For this configured checkout: `pnpm db:up`, `pnpm db:migrate`, then `pnpm dev`. Open http://localhost:3000. To verify, install Chromium once with `pnpm exec playwright install chromium`, then run `pnpm verify`. Ports and configuration details are in [README](../README.md).

## Public-beta remediation, local gate complete (2026-10-30)

Owner-authorized R01–R17 remediation targets an open-registration Calendar/Fitness beta. Existing work/data and preview implementations were retained. No commit, push, deployment, paid provisioning or production data operation was performed. FIRST_RELEASE_AUDIT.md remains historical; FIRST_RELEASE_REMEDIATION.md is the per-finding status/evidence report and PUBLIC_BETA_DEPLOYMENT.md is the operational handoff.

- Patched compatible runtime/tool dependencies and disabled compressed JSON parsing; isolated malformed compressed-body tests verify process survival. Production-only and complete dependency scans report zero known advisories. Frozen offline install succeeds.
- Added Identity-owned verification/reset/change with generic mail responses, hashed expiring single-use tokens, consistent transaction locks, session revocation and Resend delivery. Browser forms remove token fragments immediately. Provider latency is decoupled from generic responses; production needs configured sender/key. Public signup requires real verification in every environment; isolated fixtures capture email and exercise real verification. No test routes exist in production createApp.
- Unified core/preview gates cover Goals, Routines, Unscheduled, Templates, Programs, Tasks, Food, Assistant and legacy cardio entry points, preserving internal services/history. Core shared ranges/search filter unscheduled data before pagination.
- Deduplicated cross-week Calendar command identities, preserved exact unchanged saved DST instants, retained date-picker target width, and added owner/tab-scoped exact pending-write recovery for plans and manual time. Existing Undo/Redo owns its own durable inverse commands. Completion-status failures retain records with explicit retry and conservative plan emphasis.
- Retained loaded exercise history on pagination/refresh failure; limited visible weekly totals to one pair of aggregates; made comparison reads one repeatable-read snapshot; corrected result-measurement containment and restored contextual measurement selections. Fixed deterministic/stale fixtures while preserving strict data, geometry and navigation assertions.
- Production HTML uses fresh nonce-based script CSP and dynamic rendering. Browser validation is initialized without JIT to avoid an eval probe; CSP was not relaxed. Built-server testing also found and fixed the focused password input covering its visibility button. Security headers, production cookies, restricted roles, health/readiness and graceful shutdown retain their established boundaries.

Actual final checks: zero-warning lint/import boundaries; formatting/whitespace; workspace typechecks; **191 unit tests and six architecture tests**; **160/160 integration tests in 34 files**, with two complete green runs and final API run after email-timing/tooling corrections. **103/103 beta browser cases** passed in one complete desktop/phone/targeted-tablet run; **18/18 final focused browser cases** then passed after adding manual-time recovery (the beta config now collects 105 cases). Final production builds passed, followed by **3/3 production-web cases** for account lifecycle, the complete scheduled-workout journey and enforced CSP/nonces/interactive controls, including explicit error/violation checks. Accessibility/keyboard/geometry checks are included in those workflows. Narrow-phone summary and desktop focus screenshots were reviewed. Development-only React/Next Performance.measure timestamp warnings appeared without workflow failures and were not reproduced by the production smoke.

Local operational rehearsal: 39 migrations on a new disposable database and idempotent rerun; custom-format logical dump/restore into a second database; successful login, owned Calendar and completed Fitness-result reads; zero invalid indexes; restricted-role startup/read with DDL denied. The built production API then passed readiness, verified login with Secure/HttpOnly cookies, owned history, core API denial and clean SIGTERM. Temporary databases, role and dump were removed. This is not evidence of encrypted off-host production backups, retention, real email delivery or a verified public TLS edge.

Assessment: **READY FOR DEPLOYMENT TESTING**, with R04 operational acceptance blocked on owner infrastructure/credentials. R13 presentation refinements and R15 broad controller refactoring are explicitly deferred. Real hosting, sender DNS, restricted production identities, monitoring, encrypted backups/restores, operator/support process and public-host acceptance remain necessary before launch. No public-launch safety claim is made. `pnpm verify:beta` provides the ordered application gate; the separate production-web command and required build origin are documented in verification.md.
